Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in model theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 426 and the invariant I(A,B) = 89 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 426 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the model structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 89. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
